# Prebuild report: every indexable page (branch seo-prebuild)

Source: the built HTML from the prebuild (`.next` output) joined to the approved keyword map. 3007 indexable URLs. Full per-URL data with every count: `docs/seo/PREBUILD-FULL-REPORT.csv` (one row per URL, 35 columns). Nothing is committed.

**Important:** the prebuild so far applies the keyword map to the **277 category, brand and collection pages** (Batch 1). The home page, static pages, 2515 product pages and 200 blog posts are still the existing pages, so their titles, meta descriptions and keyword counts below show what is live in the build today, not the mapped keywords. Those are the next batches.

Slug note: all URLs/slugs are unchanged (the keyword is not forced into existing slugs). The "primary in slug" column in the CSV from the mapping report shows where the slug already contains it.

## 2. How to read the per-page tables

Each row: slug (URL) | page | primary keyword [intent volume/KD] (name-based = no Semrush data) | SEO title | meta description | primary counts: title/meta/H1/H2-H3/first 10%/FAQ/alt/body and density | secondary keywords [intent vol/KD] with body count and where found (M=meta, H=H2/H3, F=FAQ, A=image alt).
